### Calibration Verification Note: `person/1018.json` (`document_type`)

The Stage 1 prompt and `RECONCILIATION_PLAN_2026-11-22.md` listed `person 1018 document_type` (`group_photo -> portrait`) as a Category B calibration example. Cross-checking git history and `origin/main`'s own `photos.json` shows the opposite direction:
- Commit `7c990d6` (2026-11-11, PR #16, *Phase 4 photo metadata, scoped regen*) updated the **SQLite database**, `photos.json`, `surnames/*.json`, and `transcriptions/*.json`, setting photo `241` to `document_type: "portrait"` and photo `2675` to `document_type: "photograph_other"`, while **intentionally skipping `person/*.json`** to preserve October hygiene fixes.
- On `origin/main`, `photos.json` already has `241 -> portrait` and `2675 -> photograph_other` (0 differences vs. DB across all 2,855 common photos), whereas `person/1018.json` still carries the stale pre-Phase-4 values (`group_photo` / `portrait`).
- Classifying `person 1018` (and all `1,196` matching Phase 4 photo-metadata field diffs + `2` sort-order shifts across `person/*.json`) as **Category C (Database-ahead)** prevents Stage 2 from mistakenly reverting `7c990d6` in the database.

## 5. Category E — Exporter Schema Drift & Omitted Static Files

1. **Class `flag_type_audit_flag_schema` (45 `person/*.json` files: 33 `birth_after_death`, 12 `lifespan_over_110`):**
   - Persists under current-main exporter parity because `export_static_build_for_vercel.py` queries `audit_flags` columns (`id`, `category`, `severity`, `person_id`, `person_id_secondary`, `description`, `evidence`, `created_at`), whereas `2d37846` injected `{category, flag_type, severity, description}` directly into JSON. All 45 `date_anomaly` entries match 1-to-1 on `(person_id, category, severity, description)`.
   - Example 1 (`frontend/public/api/person/1033.json`, `$.audit_flags[category=date_anomaly]`): DB emits `{"id": 7556, "category": "date_anomaly", "severity": "critical", "person_id": 1033, ...}`; export has `{"category": "date_anomaly", "flag_type": "birth_after_death", "severity": "critical", ...}`.
   - Example 2 (`frontend/public/api/person/1039.json`, `$.audit_flags[category=date_anomaly]`): DB emits `{"id": 7557, "category": "date_anomaly", "severity": "warning", "person_id": 1039, ...}`; export has `{"category": "date_anomaly", "flag_type": "lifespan_over_110", "severity": "warning", ...}`.
2. **Class `exporter_omitted_static_and_legacy_files` (9 repo-only files):**
   - `frontend/public/api/face_reference_bank.json` (`955a1a4cf`), `fixity_status.json` (`951bd69e4`), `layout.json` (`c57c25334`), `settlements.json` (`476c6049d`), and 5 legacy surname files (`surnames/Bantum.json`, `Copes.json`, `Cremeen.json`, `Kinyon.json`, `Pinder.json` from `f92c42f74`). `export_static_build_for_vercel.py` neither generates these files nor deletes them during an in-place run.

## 6. Category F — Derived Aggregates (Count-Level Deltas)

- **`surnames.json`**: `59` vs `59` surnames. `Counselor` `individual_count`: DB `15` vs. `origin/main` `39` (delta `+24` on export). Category breakdowns also reflect the 3 reclassified mini-tree photos (`Durham`, `Mosley`, `Pierce`).
- **`graph.json`**: `1,200` nodes and `2,060` edges on both sides (`edges` identical); `26` nodes differ only in `source_page` (`probate_sussex.htm` in DB vs `Probate.htm` in export).
- **`persons_summary.json`**: `3,789` vs `3,789` persons (`has_obituary`: `92` vs `92`; `has_citations`: `3,789` vs `3,789`). `has_photo`: DB `1,320` vs export `1,324` (`14` rows differ on `has_photo`, `33` on `primary_photo`). `23` `Counselor -> Conselor` profiles have `Conselor` in DB vs `Counselor` in `origin/main`'s `persons_summary.json`.
- **`records_catalog.json`**: `334` vs `334` records; `1` entry (`probate_sussex.htm`) differs in its generated `snippet` (`Probate.htm` vs `probate_sussex.htm`).
- **`photos.json`**: DB `2,855` vs `origin/main` `2,875` (delta `+20`: PRs `#21`–`#33` photo IDs `3677`, `3679`–`3697`; `0` field differences across all `2,855` common photos). Category counts: DB `people=1,865, documents=546, family_trees=382, tombstones=62`; export `people=1,885, documents=546, family_trees=382, tombstones=62`.
